# GB(3,3) classification

Generalized bicycle codes GB(3,3): all pairs (A, B) of polynomials over GF(2) of weight 3 giving a code with parameters `[[N, k, d]]`, `N = 2n`, grouped by length `n` and then by parameters.

Each length `n` lists its best parameters: for each dimension `k`, the parameters of greatest distance `d`.

<details>

```
(X^2 + X + 1, X^5 + X + 1)

```

</details>
